# Elements of Project Management: question papers in full

46 unique papers · 2021-22 to 2025-26 · Common (all branches), Computer Engineering, Computer Science, Data Science, Electronics and Telecommunication, Information Technology, Mechanical, AI and Machine Learning, Artificial Intelligence, Cyber Security

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

Qs [20]
CO-1; a. | Case study: Read the following case study on Project management and answer the below | [10]
me mentioned questions.

Imagine you are a project manager at an educational software company. You’re assigned
a new project to develop a digital grading platform for a local high school. Before
beginning the project, you meet with teachers, school administrators, the school IT
department, and the district superintendent to discuss the project and get their input.
During these meetings, you organized your thoughts by writing down project key
components that the stakeholders have requested. Your notes on the key components are:
e $150,000 maximum budget
e Team: can add one member from the school IT department
e Platform should allow teachers to enter grades and allow students/parents to
view grades
e Need full teacher buy-in at high school: 100% adoption within next nine months
e Project should focus only on the digital grading platform and NOT impact other
digital platforms (ex. attendance, school lunch payments)
Overall, the school is seeking a platform for digital grading. The next step in the project
is to organize the key components into a project charter. You will then present the charter
to the individuals involved with the project. The project charter ensures everyone is

aligned before planning and then executing the project.

Q.1 Discuss the key components as the project’s goal.

Q.2 Discuss the component that outlines the project’s scope.

Q.3 Name the components as the project deliverable.

Q.4 Name key components that outlines the project’s success criteria.

Q.5 Discuss the components that outline the resources you will have at your disposal

for the project.
